```
    99→
   100→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   101→    *,
   102→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   103→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   104→) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   106→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   107→    seen: set[str] = set()
   108→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   109→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   110→            continue
   111→        batch = batches[idx]
   112→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   113→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   114→            continue
   115→        for raw in files:
   116→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   117→                continue
   118→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   119→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   120→                continue
   121→            if path.endswith("/"):
   122→                continue
   123→            if path in seen:
   124→                continue
   125→            seen.add(path)
   126→            reviewed.append(path)
   127→    return reviewed
   128→
   129→
   130→def _coerce_positive_int(value: object, *, default: int, minimum: int = 1) -> int:
   131→    """Parse positive integer CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   132→    if value is None:
   133→        return default
   134→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   135→        return default
   136→    try:
   137→        parsed = int(value)
   138→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   139→        return default
   140→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   141→
   142→
   143→def _coerce_positive_float(value: object, *, default: float, minimum: float = 0.1) -> float:
   144→    """Parse positive float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   145→    if value is None:
   146→        return default
   147→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   148→        return default
   149→    try:
   150→        parsed = float(value)
   151→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   152→        return default
   153→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   154→
   155→
   156→def _coerce_non_negative_float(value: object, *, default: float) -> float:
   157→    """Parse non-negative float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   158→    if value is None:
   159→        return default
   160→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   161→        return default
   162→    try:
   163→        parsed = float(value)
   164→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   165→        return default
   166→    return parsed if parsed >= 0.0 else default
   167→
   168→
   169→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   170→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   171→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   172→        return []
   173→    out: list[str] = []
   174→    seen: set[str] = set()
   175→    for item in raw:
   176→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   177→            continue
   178→        dim = item.strip()
   179→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   180→            continue
   181→        seen.add(dim)
   182→        out.append(dim)
   183→    return out
   184→
   185→
   186→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   187→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   188→    try:
   189→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   190→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   191→        return []
   192→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)
   193→
   194→
   195→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   196→    *,
   197→    selected_dims: list[str],
   198→    scored_dims: list[str],
   199→) -> list[str]:
   200→    selected = set(selected_dims)
   201→    return [dim for dim in scored_dims if dim not in selected]
   202→
   203→
   204→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   205→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   206→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   207→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   208→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   209→    if missing_dims:
   210→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"
   211→    return base
   212→
   213→
   214→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   215→    *,
   216→    selected_dims: list[str],
   217→    scored_dims: list[str],
   218→    explicit_selection: bool,
   219→    scan_path: str,
   220→    colorize_fn,
   221→) -> None:
   222→    """Print trigger-time notice when run scope is a scored-dimension subset."""
   223→    if not scored_dims:
   224→        return
   225→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   226→        selected_dims=selected_dims,
   227→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   228→    )
   229→    if not missing_dims:
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in selected_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   233→    scope_reason = (
   234→        "explicit --dimensions selection"
   235→        if explicit_selection
   236→        else "language default review dimension set"
   237→    )
   238→    tone = "yellow" if explicit_selection else "red"
   239→    print(
   240→        colorize_fn(
   241→            "  WARNING: this run targets "
   242→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions "
   243→            f"({scope_reason}).",
   244→            tone,
   245→        )
   246→    )
   247→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   248→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   249→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   250→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Missing from this run: {preview}", "yellow"))
   251→    print(
   252→        colorize_fn(
   253→            "  Rerun missing dimensions: "
   254→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   255→            "dim",
   256→        )
   257→    )
   258→
   259→
   260→def _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   261→    *,
   262→    assessed_dims: list[str],
   263→    scored_dims: list[str],
   264→    scan_path: str,
   265→    colorize_fn,
   266→) -> list[str]:
   267→    """Print result-time notice when merged import covers only a subset."""
   268→    if not scored_dims:
   269→        return []
   270→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   271→        selected_dims=assessed_dims,
   272→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   273→    )
   274→    if not missing_dims:
   275→        return []
   276→
   277→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in assessed_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   278→    print(
   279→        colorize_fn(
   280→            "  Coverage gap: imported assessments for "
   281→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions.",
   282→            "yellow",
   283→        )
   284→    )
   285→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   286→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   287→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   288→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Still missing: {preview}", "yellow"))
   289→    print(
   290→        colorize_fn(
   291→            "  Run to cover missing dimensions: "
   292→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   293→            "dim",
   294→        )
   295→    )
   296→    return missing_dims
   297→
   298→
   299→def do_run_batches(
   300→    args,
   301→    state,
   302→    lang,
   303→    state_file,
   304→    *,
   305→    config: dict | None,
   306→    run_stamp_fn,
   307→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   308→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   309→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   310→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   311→    execute_batches_fn,
   312→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   313→    print_failures_fn,
   314→    print_failures_and_exit_fn,
   315→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   316→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   317→    do_import_fn,
   318→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   319→    safe_write_text_fn,
   320→    colorize_fn,
   321→    project_root: Path,
   322→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   323→) -> None:
   324→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   325→    config = config or {}
   326→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   327→    _validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   328→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   329→    run_parallel = bool(getattr(args, "parallel", False))
   330→    max_parallel_batches = _coerce_positive_int(
   331→        getattr(args, "max_parallel_batches", None),
   332→        default=3,
   333→        minimum=1,
   334→    )
   335→    heartbeat_seconds = _coerce_positive_float(
   336→        getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
   337→        default=15.0,
   338→        minimum=0.1,
   339→    )
   340→    batch_timeout_seconds = _coerce_positive_int(
   341→        getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
   342→        default=2 * 60 * 60,
   343→        minimum=1,
   344→    )
   345→    batch_max_retries = _coerce_positive_int(
   346→        getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
   347→        default=1,
   348→        minimum=0,
   349→    )
   350→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = _coerce_non_negative_float(
   351→        getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
   352→        default=2.0,
   353→    )
   354→    raw_stall_warning = getattr(args, "batch_stall_warning_seconds", None)
   355→    stall_warning_seconds = 0
   356→    if isinstance(raw_stall_warning, int | float | str):
   357→        try:
   358→            parsed_stall_warning = int(raw_stall_warning)
   359→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   360→            parsed_stall_warning = 0
   361→        stall_warning_seconds = parsed_stall_warning if parsed_stall_warning > 0 else 0
   362→    raw_stall_kill = getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None)
   363→    stall_kill_seconds = 0
   364→    if isinstance(raw_stall_kill, int | float | str):
   365→        try:
   366→            parsed_stall_kill = int(raw_stall_kill)
   367→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   368→            parsed_stall_kill = 0
   369→        stall_kill_seconds = parsed_stall_kill if parsed_stall_kill > 0 else 0
   370→
   371→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   372→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   373→        args,
   374→        state=state,
   375→        lang=lang,
   376→        config=config,
   377→        stamp=stamp,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   381→    packet_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   382→    scored_dimensions = _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   383→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   384→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   385→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   386→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   387→        scan_path=scan_path,
   388→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   389→    )
   390→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   391→    batches = _require_batches(
   392→        packet,
   393→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   394→        suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
   395→    )
   396→
   397→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   398→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   399→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   400→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   401→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
   402→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
   403→    print(
   404→        colorize_fn(
   405→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   406→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   407→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   408→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   409→            "dim",
   410→        )
   411→    )
   412→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   413→        stamp=stamp,
   414→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   415→        batches=batches,
   416→        packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   417→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   418→        repo_root=project_root,
   419→    )
   420→    raw_run_log_file = getattr(args, "run_log_file", None)
   421→    run_log_file_value = (
   422→        raw_run_log_file.strip()
   423→        if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip()
   424→        else None
   425→    )
   426→    if run_log_file_value is not None:
   427→        candidate = Path(run_log_file_value).expanduser()
   428→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   429→    else:
   430→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   431→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   432→
   433→    def _append_run_log(message: str) -> None:
   434→        line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
   435→        try:
   436→            with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   437→                handle.write(line)
   438→        except OSError:
   439→            return
   440→
   441→    _append_run_log(
   442→        "run-start "
   443→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
   444→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
   445→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   446→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   447→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   448→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   449→    )
   450→    _append_run_log(f"run-path {run_dir}")
   451→    _append_run_log(f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   452→    _append_run_log(f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   453→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   454→
   455→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   456→        print(
   457→            colorize_fn(
   458→                "  Dry run only: prompts generated, runner execution skipped.", "yellow"
   459→            )
   460→        )
   461→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   462→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   463→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   464→        _append_run_log("run-finished dry-run")
   465→        return
   466→
   467→    def _run_batch(*, prompt: str, output_file: Path, log_file: Path) -> int:
   468→        return run_codex_batch_fn(
   469→            prompt=prompt,
   470→            repo_root=project_root,
   471→            output_file=output_file,
   472→            log_file=log_file,
   473→        )
   474→
   475→    def _make_task(batch_index: int):
   476→        prompt_path = prompt_files[batch_index]
   477→        output_path = output_files[batch_index]
   478→        log_path = log_files[batch_index]
   479→
   480→        def _task() -> int:
   481→            try:
   482→                prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   483→            except OSError as exc:
   484→                raise RuntimeError(
   485→                    f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   486→                ) from exc
   487→            return _run_batch(prompt=prompt, output_file=output_path, log_file=log_path)
   488→
   489→        return _task
   490→
   491→    tasks = {idx: _make_task(idx) for idx in selected_indexes}
   492→
   493→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   494→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   495→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   496→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
   497→        str(idx + 1): {
   498→            "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   499→            "status": "pending",
   500→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   501→            "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   502→            "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   503→        }
   504→        for idx in selected_indexes
   505→    }
   506→
   507→    if run_parallel:
   508→        print(
   509→            colorize_fn(
   510→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   511→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   512→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   513→                "dim",
   514→            )
   515→        )
   516→
   517→    def _report_progress(
   518→        batch_index: int,
   519→        event: str,
   520→        code: int | None = None,
   521→        **details,
   522→    ) -> None:
   523→        if event == "heartbeat":
   524→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   525→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   526→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   527→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   528→                active = []
   529→            if not isinstance(queued, list):
   530→                queued = []
   531→            if not active and not queued:
   532→                return
   533→            segments: list[str] = []
   534→            for idx in active[:6]:
   535→                secs = 0
   536→                if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   537→                    raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   538→                    secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   539→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   540→            if len(active) > 6:
   541→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   542→            queued_segment = ""
   543→            if queued:
   544→                queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   545→            print(
   546→                colorize_fn(
   547→                    "  Batch heartbeat: "
   548→                    f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   549→                    f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   550→                    "dim",
   551→                )
   552→            )
   553→            _append_run_log(
   554→                "heartbeat "
   555→                f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   556→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   557→            )
   558→            if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   559→                slow_active = [
   560→                    idx
   561→                    for idx in active
   562→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   563→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   564→                ]
   565→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   566→                if newly_warned:
   567→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   568→                    warning_message = (
   569→                        "  Stall warning: batches "
   570→                        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   571→                        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   572→                        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   573→                    )
   574→                    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   575→                    _append_run_log(
   576→                        "stall-warning "
   577→                        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   578→                    )
   579→            return
   580→
   581→        position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   582→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   583→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   584→            key,
   585→            {
   586→                "position": position,
   587→                "status": "pending",
   588→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   589→                "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   590→                "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   591→            },
   592→        )
   593→        if event == "queued":
   594→            state["status"] = "queued"
   595→            print(
   596→                colorize_fn(
   597→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
   598→                    "dim",
   599→                )
   600→            )
   601→            _append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   602→            return
   603→        if event == "start":
   604→            state["status"] = "running"
   605→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   606→            print(
   607→                colorize_fn(
   608→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   609→                    "dim",
   610→                )
   611→            )
   612→            _append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   613→            return
   614→        if event == "done":
   615→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   616→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   617→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   618→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   619→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   620→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   621→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   622→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   623→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   624→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   625→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   626→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   627→            print(
   628→                colorize_fn(
   629→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   630→                    tone,
   631→                )
   632→            )
   633→            _append_run_log(
   634→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   635→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   636→            )
   637→
   638→    def _record_execution_issue(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   639→        if batch_index < 0:
   640→            _append_run_log(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
   641→            return
   642→        _append_run_log(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   643→
   644→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   645→
   646→    def _write_run_summary(
   647→        *,
   648→        successful_batches: list[int],
   649→        failed_batches: list[int],
   650→        interrupted: bool = False,
   651→        interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   652→    ) -> None:
   653→        run_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   654→            "created_at": summary_created_at,
   655→            "run_stamp": stamp,
   656→            "runner": runner,
   657→            "parallel": run_parallel,
   658→            "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   659→            "successful_batches": successful_batches,
   660→            "failed_batches": failed_batches,
   661→            "allow_partial": allow_partial,
   662→            "max_parallel_batches": max_parallel_batches if run_parallel else 1,
   663→            "batch_timeout_seconds": batch_timeout_seconds,
   664→            "batch_max_retries": batch_max_retries,
   665→            "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   666→            "batch_heartbeat_seconds": heartbeat_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   667→            "batch_stall_warning_seconds": stall_warning_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   668→            "batch_stall_kill_seconds": stall_kill_seconds,
   669→            "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   670→            "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   671→            "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   672→            "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   673→            "run_log": str(run_log_path),
   674→            "batches": batch_status,
   675→        }
   676→        if interrupted:
   677→            run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   678→            if interruption_reason:
   679→                run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   680→        safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   681→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {run_summary_path}", "dim"))
   682→        _append_run_log(f"run-summary {run_summary_path}")
   683→
   684→    try:
   685→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   686→            tasks=tasks,
   687→            run_parallel=run_parallel,
   688→            progress_fn=_report_progress,
   689→            error_log_fn=_record_execution_issue,
   690→            max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   691→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   692→        )
   693→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   694→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   695→            key = str(idx + 1)
   696→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   697→                key,
   698→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   699→            )
   700→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   701→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   702→        _write_run_summary(
   703→            successful_batches=[],
   704→            failed_batches=[],
   705→            interrupted=True,
   706→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   707→        )
   708→        _append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   709→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   710→
   711→    allowed_dims = {
   712→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   713→    }
   714→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   715→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   716→        failures=execution_failures,
   717→        output_files=output_files,
   718→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   719→    )
   720→
   721→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   722→    failure_set = set(failures)
   723→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   724→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   725→        key = str(idx + 1)
   726→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   727→            key,
   728→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   729→        )
   730→        if idx not in failure_set:
   731→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   732→            continue
   733→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   734→            state["status"] = "failed"
   735→            continue
   736→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   737→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   738→            continue
   739→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   740→
   741→    _write_run_summary(
   742→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   743→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   744→    )
   745→
   746→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   747→        _append_run_log(
   748→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   749→        )
   750→        print_failures_and_exit_fn(
   751→            failures=failures,
   752→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   753→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   754→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   755→        )
   756→    elif failures:
   757→        print(
   758→            colorize_fn(
   759→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   760→                "yellow",
   761→            )
   762→        )
   763→        print_failures_fn(
   764→            failures=failures,
   765→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   766→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   767→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   768→        )
   769→        _append_run_log(
   770→            "run-partial "
   771→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   772→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   773→        )
   774→
   775→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   776→    reviewed_files = _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   777→        batches=batches,
   778→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   779→    )
   780→    full_sweep_included = any(
   781→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   782→        for idx in successful_indexes
   783→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   784→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   785→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   786→    )
   787→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   788→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   789→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   790→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   791→    }
   792→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   793→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   794→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   795→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   796→    if reviewed_files:
   797→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   798→        print(
   799→            colorize_fn(
   800→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   801→                "dim",
   802→            )
   803→        )
   804→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   805→        runner=runner,
   806→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   807→        run_stamp=stamp,
   808→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   809→    )
   810→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   811→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   812→    )
   813→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   814→        [
   815→            finding.get("dimension")
   816→            for finding in (merged.get("findings") or [])
   817→            if isinstance(finding, dict)
   818→        ]
   819→    )
   820→    merged_imported_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   821→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_finding_dims
   822→    )
   823→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   824→    missing_after_import = _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   825→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   826→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   827→        scan_path=scan_path,
   828→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   829→    )
   830→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   831→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   832→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   833→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   834→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   835→    }
   836→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   837→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   838→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   839→    _print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   840→
   841→    try:
   842→        do_import_fn(
   843→            str(merged_path),
   844→            state,
   845→            lang,
   846→            state_file,
   847→            config=config,
   848→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   849→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   850→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   851→        )
   852→    except SystemExit as exc:
   853→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   854→        raise
   855→    except Exception as exc:
   856→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   857→        raise
   858→    _append_run_log(
   859→        "run-finished "
   860→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   861→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   862→    )
   863→
   864→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   865→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   866→            lang_name=lang.name,
   867→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   868→        )
   869→        if followup_code != 0:
   870→            print(
   871→                colorize_fn(
   872→                    f"  Follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   873→                    "red",
   874→                ),
   875→                file=sys.stderr,
   876→            )
   877→            raise SystemExit(followup_code)
   878→
   879→
   880→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   881→
```

```
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):

    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():

    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()

    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats

    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:

    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:

    59→        score = 0.0

   103→        return subjective_assessments, meta

   143→def _active_scan_coverage(state: StateModel) -> ScanCoverageRecord:

   159→def _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(

   180→        confidence = coerce_confidence(raw.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   181→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:

   183→        reduced_detectors[str(detector)] = dict(raw)
   184→
   185→    reduced_dimensions: list[str] = []

   187→    for detector, payload in reduced_detectors.items():

   192→                "confidence": round(
   193→                    coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0), 2
   194→                ),
   195→                "summary": str(payload.get("summary", "") or ""),
   196→                "impact": str(payload.get("impact", "") or ""),
   197→                "remediation": str(payload.get("remediation", "") or ""),
   198→                "tool": str(payload.get("tool", "") or ""),
   199→                "reason": str(payload.get("reason", "") or ""),
   200→            }
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    for dim_name, dim_data in dimension_scores.items():
   204→        if not isinstance(dim_data, dict):
   205→            continue
   206→        detectors = dim_data.get("detectors", {})
   207→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   208→            continue
   209→
   210→        impacts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   211→        for detector_name, detector_meta in detectors.items():
   212→            reduced = reduced_detectors.get(str(detector_name))
   213→            if not isinstance(detector_meta, dict):
   214→                continue
   215→            if reduced is None:
   216→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_status", None)
   217→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   218→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_summary", None)
   219→                continue
   220→            confidence = coerce_confidence(reduced.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   221→            status = str(reduced.get("status", "reduced"))
   222→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   223→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   224→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   226→            impacts.append(
   227→                {
   228→                    "detector": str(detector_name),
   229→                    "status": status,
   230→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   231→                    "summary": summary,
   232→                }
   233→            )
   234→
   235→        if not impacts:
   236→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   237→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   238→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   239→            continue
   240→
   241→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   242→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   243→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(
   244→            min(coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0) for item in impacts),
   245→            2,
   246→        )
   247→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts
   248→
   249→    if not score_confidence_detectors:
   250→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   251→            "status": "full",
   252→            "confidence": 1.0,
   253→            "detectors": [],
   254→            "dimensions": [],
   255→        }
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    state["score_confidence"] = {
   259→        "status": "reduced",
   260→        "confidence": round(
   261→            min(
   262→                coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   263→                for item in score_confidence_detectors
   264→            ),
   265→            2,
   266→        ),
   267→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,
   268→        "dimensions": sorted(set(reduced_dimensions)),
   269→    }
   270→
   271→
   272→def _update_objective_health(
   273→    state: StateModel,
   274→    findings: dict,
   275→    *,
   276→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   277→) -> None:
   278→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   279→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   280→    if not pots:
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   284→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   285→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   286→        compute_health_score,
   287→        compute_score_bundle,
   288→        merge_potentials,
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   292→    if not merged:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   296→    integrity_target = (
   297→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   298→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   299→        else None
   300→    )
   301→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   302→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   303→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   304→            subjective_assessments,
   305→            target=integrity_target,
   306→        )
   307→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   308→
   309→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   310→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   311→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   312→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   313→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   314→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   316→        return
   317→
   318→    # Derive language-scoped subjective dimensions so only dimensions with
   319→    # a review pathway get 0-score placeholders.  Explicit assessments
   320→    # outside this set still count (append_subjective_dimensions handles
   321→    # that).  Without this, all 20 global default dimensions would get
   322→    # placeholders — including ones the language's review never covers.
   323→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   324→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   325→    if lang_name:
   326→        try:
   327→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.lang import (
   328→                HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG,
   329→            )
   330→
   331→            lang_dims = HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG.get(lang_name)
   332→            if lang_dims:
   333→                allowed_subjective = set(lang_dims)
   334→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   335→            pass
   336→
   337→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   338→        findings,
   339→        merged,
   340→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   341→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   342→    )
   343→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   344→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   345→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   346→
   347→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   348→
   349→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   350→        name: dict(
   351→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   352→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   355→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   356→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   357→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   358→        )
   359→        for name in lenient_scores
   360→    }
   361→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   362→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   363→
   364→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   365→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   366→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   367→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   368→            continue
   369→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   370→            continue
   371→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   372→            continue
   373→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   374→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   375→        carried.setdefault(
   376→            "verified_strict_score",
   377→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   378→        )
   379→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   380→
   381→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   382→        state,
   383→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   387→
   388→
   389→def _recompute_stats(
   390→    state: StateModel,
   391→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   392→    *,
   393→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   396→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   397→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   398→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   399→    state["stats"] = {
   400→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   401→        **counters,
   402→        "by_tier": {
   403→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   404→        },
   405→    }
   406→    _update_objective_health(
   407→        state,
   408→        findings,
   409→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   410→    )
   411→
   412→
   413→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   414→    return {
   415→        "last_ignored": 0,
   416→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   417→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   418→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   419→        "recent_scans": 0,
   420→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   421→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   422→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   423→    }
   424→
   425→
   426→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   427→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   428→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   429→    if not history:
   430→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   431→
   432→    scans_with_suppression = [
   433→        entry
   434→        for entry in history
   435→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   436→        and (
   437→            "ignored" in entry
   438→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   439→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   440→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   441→        )
   442→    ]
   443→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   444→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   445→
   446→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   447→    last = recent[-1]
   448→
   449→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   450→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   451→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   452→
   453→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   454→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   455→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   456→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   457→    else:
   458→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   459→
   460→    return {
   461→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   462→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   463→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   464→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   465→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   466→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   467→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   468→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   469→    }
   470→
```

```
1→"""Load and validate review dimension payloads from JSON data files."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→import json
     7→from functools import lru_cache
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import ensure_prompt_contract
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.validation import (
    12→    parse_dimensions_payload,
    13→)
    14→
    15→_LANG_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "languages"
    16→_LANG_DATA_SUBDIR = "review_data"
    17→_DATA_DIR = _LANG_DIR / "_framework" / _LANG_DATA_SUBDIR
    18→
    19→# Canonical filename for the unified dimensions payload.
    20→_DIMENSIONS_FILE = "dimensions.json"
    21→
    22→
    23→def _load_json_payload_from_path(path: Path) -> dict:
    24→    """Load a JSON payload from *path* and return a dict."""
    25→    try:
    26→        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    27→    except OSError as exc:
    28→        raise RuntimeError(f"Unable to read dimensions payload: {path}") from exc
    29→
    30→    try:
    31→        payload = json.loads(raw)
    32→    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
    33→        raise ValueError(f"Invalid JSON in dimensions payload: {path}") from exc
    34→
    35→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    36→        raise ValueError(f"Dimensions payload must be a JSON object: {path}")
    37→    return payload
    38→
    39→
    40→def _lang_payload_path(lang_name: str, filename: str) -> Path:
    41→    """Resolve the per-language review payload path."""
    42→    return _LANG_DIR / lang_name / _LANG_DATA_SUBDIR / filename
    43→
    44→
    45→def _override_filename(filename: str) -> str:
    46→    """Convert ``foo.json`` to ``foo.override.json``."""
    47→    if not filename.endswith(".json"):
    48→        return f"{filename}.override.json"
    49→    return f"{filename[:-5]}.override.json"
    50→
    51→
    52→def _load_json_payload(filename: str) -> dict:
    53→    """Load a JSON payload from the shared language review-data directory."""
    54→    return _load_json_payload_from_path(_DATA_DIR / filename)
    55→
    56→
    57→def _validate_optional_string_list(value: object, *, context: str) -> list[str]:
    58→    """Validate an optional list of strings (empty allowed)."""
    59→    if value is None:
    60→        return []
    61→    if not isinstance(value, list):
    62→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be a list of strings")
    63→    out: list[str] = []
    64→    for idx, item in enumerate(value):
    65→        if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():
    66→            raise ValueError(f"{context}[{idx}] must be a non-empty string")
    67→        out.append(item)
    68→    return out
    69→
    70→
    71→def _apply_dimensions_override(
    72→    base_payload: dict,
    73→    override_payload: dict,
    74→    *,
    75→    dims_key: str,
    76→    context: str,
    77→) -> dict:
    78→    """Apply a language override payload to a base dimensions payload."""
    79→    if not isinstance(override_payload, dict):
    80→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be a JSON object")
    81→
    82→    allowed = {
    83→        dims_key,
    84→        f"{dims_key}_append",
    85→        f"{dims_key}_remove",
    86→        "dimension_prompts",
    87→        "dimension_prompts_remove",
    88→        "system_prompt",
    89→        "system_prompt_append",
    90→    }
    91→    actual = set(override_payload)
    92→    extra = sorted(actual - allowed)
    93→    if extra:
    94→        raise ValueError(f"{context} has unsupported keys: {extra}")
    95→
    96→    out = copy.deepcopy(base_payload)
    97→
    98→    if dims_key in override_payload:
    99→        out[dims_key] = override_payload[dims_key]
   100→
   101→    dims = list(out.get(dims_key, []))
   102→    for dim in _validate_optional_string_list(
   103→        override_payload.get(f"{dims_key}_append"),
   104→        context=f"{context}.{dims_key}_append",
   105→    ):
   106→        if dim not in dims:
   107→            dims.append(dim)
   108→
   109→    remove_dims = set(
   110→        _validate_optional_string_list(
   111→            override_payload.get(f"{dims_key}_remove"),
   112→            context=f"{context}.{dims_key}_remove",
   113→        )
   114→    )
   115→    if remove_dims:
   116→        dims = [dim for dim in dims if dim not in remove_dims]
   117→    out[dims_key] = dims
   118→
   119→    if "dimension_prompts" in override_payload:
   120→        prompt_overrides = override_payload["dimension_prompts"]
   121→        if not isinstance(prompt_overrides, dict):
   122→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.dimension_prompts must be an object")
   123→        prompts = dict(out.get("dimension_prompts", {}))
   124→        for dim_name, prompt in prompt_overrides.items():
   125→            prompts[dim_name] = prompt
   126→        out["dimension_prompts"] = prompts
   127→
   128→    remove_prompts = _validate_optional_string_list(
   129→        override_payload.get("dimension_prompts_remove"),
   130→        context=f"{context}.dimension_prompts_remove",
   131→    )
   132→    if remove_prompts:
   133→        prompts = dict(out.get("dimension_prompts", {}))
   134→        for dim_name in remove_prompts:
   135→            prompts.pop(dim_name, None)
   136→        out["dimension_prompts"] = prompts
   137→
   138→    if "system_prompt" in override_payload:
   139→        out["system_prompt"] = override_payload["system_prompt"]
   140→    if "system_prompt_append" in override_payload:
   141→        suffix = override_payload["system_prompt_append"]
   142→        if not isinstance(suffix, str):
   143→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.system_prompt_append must be a string")
   144→        current = out.get("system_prompt", "")
   145→        sep = "\n\n" if current and suffix else ""
   146→        out["system_prompt"] = f"{current}{sep}{suffix}"
   147→
   148→    return out
   149→
   150→
   151→def _load_payload_for_lang(
   152→    lang_name: str,
   153→    filename: str,
   154→    *,
   155→    dims_key: str,
   156→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   157→    """Load payload for a language using shared-base + optional language overlay.
   158→
   159→    Resolution order:
   160→    1) Start from shared base payload in ``lang/framework/review_data``.
   161→    2) If language override payload exists, patch the selected base.
   162→    """
   163→    base_payload = _load_json_payload(filename)
   164→    context = filename
   165→
   166→    lang_override_path = _lang_payload_path(lang_name, _override_filename(filename))
   167→    if lang_override_path.is_file():
   168→        override_payload = _load_json_payload_from_path(lang_override_path)
   169→        base_payload = _apply_dimensions_override(
   170→            base_payload,
   171→            override_payload,
   172→            dims_key=dims_key,
   173→            context=str(lang_override_path),
   174→        )
   175→        context = f"{context} + {lang_override_path}"
   176→
   177→    return base_payload, context
   178→
   179→
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→# Canonical loaders — use these for new code.
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→
   185→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   186→def load_dimensions() -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   187→    """Load and validate the unified review dimension definitions."""
   188→    payload = _load_json_payload(_DIMENSIONS_FILE)
   189→    dims, prompts, system_prompt = parse_dimensions_payload(
   190→        payload, context_prefix=_DIMENSIONS_FILE
   191→    )
   192→    return dims, prompts, ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt)
   193→
   194→
   195→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   196→def load_dimensions_for_lang(
   197→    lang_name: str,
   198→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   199→    """Load unified review dimensions for a language (with lang override applied)."""
   200→    payload, context = _load_payload_for_lang(
   201→        lang_name,
   202→        _DIMENSIONS_FILE,
   203→        [REDACTED],
   204→    )
   205→    dims, prompts, system_prompt = parse_dimensions_payload(
   206→        payload, context_prefix=context
   207→    )
   208→    return dims, prompts, ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt)
   209→
```

```
   241→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   242→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.

   305→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   306→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   307→    file_requests = []
   308→    for filepath in files:
   309→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   310→        if content is None:
   311→            continue
   312→
   313→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   314→        zone = "production"
   315→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
   316→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath).value
   317→
   318→        neighbors: dict
   319→        if lang.dep_graph:
   320→            entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   321→            imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
   322→            importers_raw = entry.get("importers", set())
   323→            importer_count_value = importer_count(entry)
   324→            neighbors = {
   325→                "imports": _rel_list(imports_raw),
   326→                "importers": _rel_list(importers_raw),
   327→                "importer_count": importer_count_value,
   328→            }
   329→        else:
   330→            neighbors = {}
   331→
   332→        file_requests.append(
   333→            {
   334→                "file": rpath,
   335→                "content": content,
   336→                "zone": zone,
   337→                "loc": len(content.splitlines()),
   338→                "neighbors": neighbors,
   339→                "existing_findings": get_file_findings(state, filepath),
   340→            }
   341→        )
   342→    return file_requests
   343→
   344→
   345→def prepare_holistic_review(
   346→    path: Path,
   347→    lang: object,
   348→    state: dict,
   349→    options: HolisticReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   350→) -> dict[str, object]:
   351→    """Prepare holistic review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict."""
   352→    resolved_options = options or HolisticReviewPrepareOptions()
   353→    all_files = (
   354→        resolved_options.files
   355→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   356→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   357→    )
   358→    allowed_review_files = _collect_allowed_review_files(
   359→        all_files,
   360→        lang,
   361→        base_path=path,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   365→    if not already_cached:
   366→        enable_file_cache()
   367→    try:
   368→        context = HolisticContext.from_raw(
   369→            build_holistic_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   370→        )
   371→        # Also include per-file review context for reference
   372→        review_ctx = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   373→    finally:
   374→        if not already_cached:
   375→            disable_file_cache()
   376→
   377→    default_dims, holistic_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   378→    _, per_file_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   379→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   380→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   381→        lang_name=lang.name,
   382→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   383→    )
   384→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   385→    valid_dims = set(holistic_prompts) | set(per_file_prompts)
   386→    invalid_requested = [
   387→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   388→    ]
   389→    invalid_default = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim not in valid_dims]
   390→    batches = _build_investigation_batches(
   391→        context,
   392→        lang,
   393→        repo_root=path,
   394→        max_files_per_batch=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   395→    )
   396→
   397→    # Append design-coherence batch from mechanical concern signals.
   398→    try:
   399→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   400→
   401→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang.name)
   402→        concerns = [
   403→            concern
   404→            for concern in concerns
   405→            if _file_in_allowed_scope(getattr(concern, "file", ""), allowed_review_files)
   406→        ]
   407→        concerns_batch = _batch_concerns(
   408→            concerns,
   409→            max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   410→            active_dimensions=dims,
   411→        )
   412→        if concerns_batch:
   413→            batches.append(concerns_batch)
   414→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   415→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   416→
   417→    batches = _filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   418→        batches,
   419→        dims,
```

```
1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode

    11→    DIMENSIONS,
    12→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
    13→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    14→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    15→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    16→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    17→    SCORING_MODES,
    18→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    19→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    20→    Dimension,
    21→    ScoreMode,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    24→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float
    37→
    38→
    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(

   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→

   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")

   149→    )
```

```
   101→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   102→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   103→
   104→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   105→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   106→    """
   107→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   108→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   109→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   110→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   111→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   112→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   113→            continue
   114→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   115→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   116→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   117→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   118→    return [
   119→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   120→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   121→    ]
   122→
   123→
   124→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   125→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   126→
   127→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   128→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   129→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   130→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   131→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   132→}
   133→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   134→
   135→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   136→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   137→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   138→
   139→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   140→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   141→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   142→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   143→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   144→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   145→
   146→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   147→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   148→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   149→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   150→
   151→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   152→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   153→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   154→    "file health": 2.0,
   155→    "code quality": 1.0,
   156→    "duplication": 1.0,
   157→    "test health": 1.0,
   158→    "security": 1.0,
   159→}
   160→
   161→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   162→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   163→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   164→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   165→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   166→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   167→    "contracts": 12.0,
   168→    "type safety": 12.0,
   169→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   170→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   171→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   172→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   173→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   174→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   175→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   176→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   177→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   178→}
   179→
   180→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   181→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   182→
   183→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   184→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   185→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   186→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   187→}
   188→
   189→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   190→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   191→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   192→
   193→
   194→def matches_target_score(
   195→    score: object,
   196→    target: object,
   197→    *,
   198→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   199→) -> bool:
   200→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   201→    try:
   202→        score_value = float(score)
   203→        target_value = float(target)
   204→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   205→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return False
   207→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   208→
   209→
   210→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   211→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   212→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   213→    _rebuild_derived()
   214→
   215→
   216→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   217→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   218→
   219→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   220→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   221→    """
   222→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   223→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   224→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   225→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   226→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   227→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   228→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   229→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   230→    )
   231→
   232→
   233→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   234→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   235→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   236→        detector,
   237→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→__all__ = [
   242→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",
   243→    "DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES",
   244→    "DIMENSIONS",
   245→    "DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME",
   246→    "FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE",
   247→    "FILE_BASED_DETECTORS",
   248→    "HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER",
   249→    "HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL",
   250→    "MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS",
   251→    "MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION",
   252→    "MIN_SAMPLE",
   253→    "SCORING_MODES",

   262→    "ScoreMode",

   264→    "matches_target_score",
   265→    "register_scoring_policy",
   266→]
```

```
1→"""Language-derived review dimensions and guidance helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify.languages import available_langs, get_lang
     8→
     9→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    10→
    11→
    12→def _collect_holistic_dims_by_lang() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    13→    """Collect per-language holistic dimension defaults from language plugins."""
    14→    out: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    15→    for lang_name in available_langs():
    16→        try:
    17→            dims = list(
    18→                getattr(get_lang(lang_name), "holistic_review_dimensions", [])
    19→                or []
    20→            )
    21→        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError, ImportError) as exc:
    22→            logger.debug("Skipping holistic dimensions for %s: %s", lang_name, exc)
    23→            dims = []
    24→        if dims:
    25→            out[lang_name] = dims
    26→    return out
    27→
    28→
    29→HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG: dict[str, list[str]] = _collect_holistic_dims_by_lang()
    30→
    31→
    32→def _collect_lang_guidance() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    33→    """Collect review guidance from registered language plugins."""
    34→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
    35→    for lang_name in available_langs():
    36→        try:
    37→            guide = getattr(get_lang(lang_name), "review_guidance", {}) or {}
    38→        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError, ImportError) as exc:
    39→            logger.debug("Skipping review guidance for %s: %s", lang_name, exc)
    40→            guide = {}
    41→        if guide:
    42→            out[lang_name] = guide
    43→    return out
    44→
    45→
    46→LANG_GUIDANCE: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = _collect_lang_guidance()
    47→
    48→
    49→def get_lang_guidance(lang_name: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    50→    """Return language-specific review guidance from plugin configuration."""
    51→    if lang_name in LANG_GUIDANCE:
    52→        return LANG_GUIDANCE[lang_name]
    53→
    54→    try:
    55→        guide = getattr(get_lang(lang_name), "review_guidance", {}) or {}
    56→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    57→        logger.debug("Failed to load review guidance for %s: %s", lang_name, exc)
    58→        guide = {}
    59→
    60→    if guide:
    61→        LANG_GUIDANCE[lang_name] = guide
    62→    return guide
    63→
```

```
510→
   511→def filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   512→    batches: list[dict],
   513→    dimensions: list[str],
   514→    *,
   515→    fallback_max_files: int | None = 80,
   516→) -> list[dict]:
   517→    """Keep only dimensions explicitly active for this holistic review run.
   518→
   519→    If selected dimensions are not represented by any batch mapping, append a
   520→    fallback batch over representative files so scoped runs still get guidance.
   521→    """
   522→    selected = [d for d in dimensions if isinstance(d, str) and d]
   523→    if not selected:
   524→        return []
   525→    selected_set = set(selected)
   526→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   527→    covered: set[str] = set()
   528→    for batch in batches:
   529→        batch_dims = [dim for dim in batch.get("dimensions", []) if dim in selected_set]
   530→        if not batch_dims:
   531→            continue
   532→        filtered.append({**batch, "dimensions": batch_dims})
   533→        covered.update(batch_dims)
   534→
   535→    missing = [dim for dim in selected if dim not in covered]
   536→    if not missing:
   537→        return filtered
   538→
   539→    # Keep fallback batches tractable; giant sweeps are expensive and often
   540→    # unnecessary when dimensions are already explicitly scoped.
   541→    max_files = fallback_max_files if isinstance(fallback_max_files, int) else None
   542→    if isinstance(max_files, int) and max_files <= 0:
   543→        max_files = None
   544→    fallback_files = _collect_files_from_batches(filtered or batches, max_files=max_files)
   545→    if not fallback_files:
   546→        return filtered
   547→
   548→    filtered.append(
   549→        {
   550→            "name": "Cross-cutting Sweep",
   551→            "dimensions": missing,
   552→            "files_to_read": fallback_files,
   553→            "why": "selected dimensions had no direct batch mapping; review representative cross-cutting files",
   554→        }
   555→    )
   556→    return filtered
   557→
   558→
   559→def batch_concerns(
   560→    concerns: list,
   561→    *,
   562→    max_files: int | None = None,
   563→    active_dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
   564→) -> dict | None:
   565→    """Build investigation batch from mechanical concern signals.
   566→
   567→    *concerns* should be a list of Concern dataclass instances from
   568→    ``desloppify.engine.concerns``.
   569→    """
   570→    if not concerns:
   571→        return None
   572→    default_dims = ["design_coherence", "initialization_coupling"]
   573→    selected_dims = [
   574→        dim for dim in (active_dimensions or [])
   575→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim
   576→    ]
   577→    selected_set = set(selected_dims)
   578→    overlap_dims = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim in selected_set]
   579→    concern_dims = overlap_dims or list(default_dims)
   580→    mapped_to_active_dims = bool(selected_dims) and not overlap_dims
   581→    if mapped_to_active_dims:
   582→        # Prevent concern signals from being silently dropped by scoped runs.
   583→        concern_dims = list(selected_dims)
   584→
   585→    types = sorted({c.type for c in concerns if c.type})
   586→    why_parts = ["mechanical detectors identified structural patterns needing judgment"]
   587→    if types:
   588→        why_parts.append(f"concern types: {', '.join(types)}")
   589→    if mapped_to_active_dims:
```
